# specs/000-foundation/fiscal-br.md

## Escopos Futuros

| Documento | Descrição                                                 | Status    |
| --------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | --------- |
| NFC-e     | Nota Fiscal de Consumidor Eletrônica (varejo/restaurante) | Planejado |
| SAT       | Sistema Autenticador e Transmissor de Cupons Fiscais (SP) | Planejado |
| NF-e      | Nota Fiscal Eletrônica (B2B/wholesale)                    | Planejado |

## Contingência Offline

**Requisito de PDV**: venda sem internet.

- Cache local de autorização (número de série, QR Code offline)
- Sincronização assíncrona quando conectividade retorna
- `print_job` com status `queued` → `printed` mesmo offline
- Ver `specs/packs/restaurant/contracts/restaurant-api.md` (turno de caixa)

## Retenção Fiscal Própria

Documento fiscal tem retenção de **5 anos** (Art. 173 CTN + legislação estadual).

- Cruzar com `specs/000-foundation/snapshot.md` (order_item congela tax_snapshot)
- Cruzar com `specs/001-auth-context/spec.md` §Data Governance (PII, auth_event 90d vs fiscal 5y)
- Tabelas fiscais (futuras): `fiscal_document`, `fiscal_document_item`, `fiscal_event` — retenção 5y, RLS por company+location

## Referência

Spec detalhada virá quando o núcleo (`specs/006-core-order/`) fechar.
Dependências: `party` (customer/supplier), `catalog` (product com NCM/CEST/CFOP), `inventory` (stock_movement para CIAP), `finance` (contas a pagar/receber para escrituração).


===== FILE: specs/000-foundation/idempotency.md =====

specs/000-foundation/idempotency.md: Unicode text, UTF-8 text

# specs/000-foundation/idempotency.md

## Header Obrigatório

`Idempotency-Key` obrigatório em:

- Criação de `order`
- Criação de `payment`
- Fechamento de conta (restaurante)
- Emissão fiscal (NFC-e, SAT, NF-e)
- Baixa de estoque (`stock_movement`)

## Tabela `idempotency_record`

```sql
CREATE TABLE idempotency_record (
  company_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES company(id),
  key varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  request_hash varchar(64) NOT NULL,  -- SHA-256 do body canonicalizado
  response_json jsonb NOT NULL,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (company_id, key)
);
```

Índice: `(company_id, expires_at)` para purga.

## TTL

| Operação                       | TTL |
| ------------------------------ | --- |
| PDV (order, payment, conta)    | 24h |
| Fiscal (emissão, cancelamento) | 7d  |
| Estoque (movimentação)         | 24h |

## Retry

Devolve resposta **original byte-a-byte** (status, headers, body). Cliente não precisa reprocessar.

## Cliente

Gera UUID v7 (timestamp + random) — ordenável, único, sem coordenação.


===== FILE: specs/000-foundation/money.md =====

specs/000-foundation/money.md: Unicode text, UTF-8 text

# specs/000-foundation/money.md

## Tipo Oficial

`numeric(12,2)` para valores monetários.

**Justificativa do teto (12 dígitos, 2 decimais):**

- Suporta até 999,999,999.99 (quase 1 bi) — suficiente para qualquer transação unitária no varejo/restaurante/ERP B2B.
- Alinha com NF-e/NFC-e (valor total até 15 dígitos, mas itens unitários tipicamente < 1 bi).
- `numeric` evita erros de ponto flutuante; `DECIMAL` em PG é alias de `numeric`.

## Arredondamento

**Half-up no servidor POR LINHA** (cada `order_item.unit_price * quantity` arredondado individualmente antes de somar).

```sql
-- Exemplo de arredondamento half-up por linha
SELECT round((unit_price * quantity)::numeric, 2) AS line_total
```

NUNCA arredondar só no total final — acumula diferença de centavos.

## Total = Soma das Linhas

O servidor **SEMPRE** recalcula `order.total_amount = sum(line_totals)`.
NUNCA aceitar `total_amount` enviado pelo cliente (anti-fraude, consistência).

## Quantidade

`numeric(12,3)` — permite kg fracionado em restaurante (ex.: 0.250 kg).

## Contexto Fiscal BR

- NFC-e / SAT: valor unitário com 4 casas decimais permitido, mas armazenamos 2 (arredondado half-up na linha).
- NF-e: total da nota com 2 casas.
- PIS/COFINS/ICMS calculados sobre base arredondada por item.
- Referência: Manual de Orientação do Contribuinte NFC-e, NT 2024.001.


===== FILE: specs/000-foundation/naming.md =====

specs/000-foundation/naming.md: Unicode text, UTF-8 text

# specs/000-foundation/naming.md

## Convenções

- **Tabela**: singular (`company`, `order`, `order_item`)
- **Coluna**: `snake_case`
- **JSON/JSONB**: `camelCase` nas chaves
- **PK**: `uuid` (nunca `text`)
- **UNIQUE de negócio**: sempre `(company_id, campo)` — ex.: `(company_id, slug)`

## Índice Padrão

Tabelas de movimento (`order`, `order_item`, `stock_movement`, `cash_shift`, `kitchen_ticket`, `print_job`):

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_{tabela}_tenant_time ON {tabela} (company_id, location_id, created_at DESC);
```

## Glossário

| Termo          | Significado                                                        |
| -------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `tenant`       | = `company` (não confundir com plugin organization do Better Auth) |
| `organization` | **não usado** — plugin do Better Auth desabilitado                 |
| `location`     | Unidade física (loja, restaurante, filial)                         |

## Mapeamento Explícito

| Sessão (Better Auth)       | Banco (snake_case)           |
| -------------------------- | ---------------------------- |
| `session.activeCompanyId`  | `session.active_company_id`  |
| `session.activeLocationId` | `session.active_location_id` |

Toda conversão camelCase ↔ snake_case na boundary (handler ↔ repo).


===== FILE: specs/000-foundation/rls-pattern.md =====

specs/000-foundation/rls-pattern.md: Unicode text, UTF-8 text

# specs/000-foundation/rls-pattern.md

## Template de Policy por Tipo

| Tipo            | USING                                                                                             | WITH CHECK                                                         |
| --------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| tenant-scoped   | `company_id = get_tenant_id()`                                                                    | `company_id = get_tenant_id()`                                     |
| location-scoped | `company_id = get_tenant_id() AND (location_id = get_location_id() OR get_location_id() IS NULL)` | `company_id = get_tenant_id() AND location_id = get_location_id()` |
| global          | (sem RLS / role dedicada)                                                                         | (sem RLS / role dedicada)                                          |
| user-scoped     | `user_id = get_user_id()`                                                                         | `user_id = get_user_id()`                                          |

## Matriz Tabela × Operação × Role

| Tabela         | SELECT       | INSERT       | UPDATE       | DELETE | Role         |
| -------------- | ------------ | ------------ | ------------ | ------ | ------------ |
| company        | own          | owner        | owner        | owner  | owner        |
| company_member | own+member   | owner        | owner        | owner  | owner/member |
| order          | own+location | own+location | own+location | owner  | owner/member |
| order_item     | own+location | own+location | own+location | owner  | owner/member |
| stock_movement | own+location | own+location | —            | —      | owner/member |
| catalog        | own          | owner        | owner        | owner  | owner        |
| party          | own          | own          | own          | own    | owner/member |

_own = tenant atual; location = location atual (ou null = superadmin)_

## Assinatura `withTenant`

```typescript
function withTenant<T>(
  companyId: string,
  locationId: string | null,
  userId: string,
  fn: (tx: Tx) => Promise<T>,
  existingTx?: Tx,
): Promise<T>
```

Único caminho para queries com RLS. Seta GUCs `app.tenant_id`, `app.location_id`, `app.user_id` na transação.

## GUCs Oficiais

- `app.tenant_id` (company_id)
- `app.location_id` (location_id, nullable)
- `app.user_id` (user_id)

## Fail-Closed

GUC ausente → `NULLIF` → `NULL` → 0 rows → API 404 genérico. Nunca vaza dado.

## Proibições

- `SECURITY DEFINER` functions
- `BYPASSRLS` role (exceto `merp` owner para migrações)
- `SET` session-level GUCs fora de `withTenant`
- Views sem `SECURITY INVOKER` — salvo ADR de segurança

## Cross-Tenant Legítimo

Relatórios agregados, suporte: role dedicada + policy própria + audit log obrigatório.

## ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES

Regra (referenciar migration 0020). Grants automáticos para `merp_app` em novas tabelas.

## Proibido: UPDATE com USING ≠ WITH CHECK

Sem ADR justificando. Default: USING = WITH CHECK.


===== FILE: specs/000-foundation/snapshot.md =====

specs/000-foundation/snapshot.md: Unicode text, UTF-8 text

# specs/000-foundation/snapshot.md

## Regra

Todo `order_item` **congela** no momento da criação:

| Campo              | Origem                        | Motivo                                 |
| ------------------ | ----------------------------- | -------------------------------------- |
| `product_name`     | `product.name`                | Editar produto não reescreve o passado |
| `unit_price`       | `price_list` vigente          | Histórico de preço fiel                |
| `tax_snapshot`     | `tax_rule` vigente            | Alíquota na data do pedido             |
| `bom_snapshot`     | `product.bom` (ficha técnica) | Composição no momento da venda         |
| `discount_applied` | Regra de desconto vigente     | Auditoria de margem                    |

## Motivação

- Editar produto/preço/imposto **não** reescreve pedidos passados.
- Fiscal: documento emitido reflete snapshot, não estado atual.
- Cruzar com `specs/000-foundation/money.md` (arredondamento por linha) e `specs/001-auth-context/spec.md` §Data Governance (retenção 5 anos).

## Aplicação

Vale para:

- `order_item` (núcleo)
- `fiscal_document_item` (futuro)
- Qualquer linha de movimento que gere documento fiscal


===== FILE: specs/001-auth-context/contracts/auth-api.md =====

specs/001-auth-context/contracts/auth-api.md: Unicode text, UTF-8 text, with very long lines (340)

# Auth API Contracts

Base path: `/api/auth`

**Versionamento**: endpoints de auth permanecem em `/api/auth` (exceção deliberada — contrato estável). Novos endpoints de domínio usam `/api/v1` (ver ADR-005).

All endpoints follow consistent error format:

```json
{ "error": "snake_case_code", "message": "Human-readable message" }
```

Error codes: `validation_error` (400), `unauthorized` (401), `session_expired` (401), `forbidden` (403), `email_not_verified` (403), `csrf_invalid` (403), `not_found` (404), `conflict` (409), `rate_limit_exceeded` (429), `service_unavailable` (503), `upstream_timeout` (503), `auth_unavailable` (503), `internal_error` (500)

## Auth model

- **Session cookie**: `merp-session_token` (httpOnly; `Secure` + `SameSite=Lax`).
- **Session TTL**: fixed 7d from creation (`expiresIn: 7d`, `disableSessionRefresh: true`).
  `rememberMe: true` persists the cookie for 7d; without it the cookie is session-scoped.
  The DB row always expires 7d after creation (FR-009).
- **CSRF**: all `POST` endpoints require the `x-csrf-token` header matching the
  `merp-csrf_token` cookie, **except** the public account-recovery flows:
  `register`, `login`, `forget-password`, `reset-password` (POST),
  `send-verification-email`. `GET` endpoints never require CSRF.
- **Rate limits** (sliding window, 60s): 20 req/min per IP on public mutation
  endpoints, 5 req/min per email, 60 req/min per authenticated user (FR-010, AUD-16).
  `429` responses carry `Retry-After`, `RateLimit-Reset` and `RateLimit-Remaining` headers.
- **Lockout**: 10 failed logins lock the (email, IP) pair; 50 total failures lock the
  email globally. Both auto-unlock after 30min. Locked callers receive the same generic
  `401` as invalid credentials (anti-enumeration, FR-016, AUD-02).

---

## POST /api/auth/register

Register a new user account. Public. CSRF-exempt.

**Request Body**:

```json
{
  "email": "user@example.com",
  "password": "secure-password-123",
  "name": "User Name",
  "consentTermsVersion": "v1.0"
}
```

`name` and `consentTermsVersion` (LGPD legal basis, FR-021) are required.
Passwords: min 8 / max 128 chars, rejected when on the common-password deny-list
or found in breaches (HIBP k-anonymity, NIST 800-63B).

**Success Response**: `201 Created`

```json
{
  "id": "uuid",
  "email": "user@example.com",
  "name": "User Name",
  "createdAt": "2026-06-12T00:00:00Z"
}
```

**Note**: Registration does NOT create a session per FR-001. A verification link is
emailed on sign-up (`sendOnSignUp`); login is blocked until the email is verified (AUD-04).

**Error Responses**:

| Status | Error Code          | Condition                                                                              |
| ------ | ------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 400    | validation_error    | Invalid email / short, weak or breached password / missing name or consentTermsVersion |
| 429    | rate_limit_exceeded | Rate limit per IP (20/min) or per email (5/min)                                        |
| 503    | service_unavailable | Database unreachable                                                                   |

**Note (AUD-03)**: if the email is already registered, the endpoint returns the same generic `201 Created` response (id/name/createdAt derived from the request input) — no `409 conflict`, no observable difference between new and existing emails (anti-enumeration). No duplicate account is created and the existing account is never modified.

---

## POST /api/auth/login

Authenticate with email and password. Creates a session (FR-005). Public. CSRF-exempt.

**Request Body**:

```json
{
  "email": "user@example.com",
  "password": "secure-password-123",
  "rememberMe": false
}
```

**Success Response**: `200 OK` — Session cookie (`Set-Cookie`) set automatically.

```json
{
  "id": "uuid",
  "email": "user@example.com",
  "name": "User Name"
}
```

**Error Responses**:

| Status | Error Code          | Condition                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ------ | ------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 400    | validation_error    | Missing email or password                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| 401    | unauthorized        | Invalid credentials (generic — no distinction between wrong password, non-existent user, locked, or deactivated account per CHK007; a deactivation notice email is sent to the account owner instead, AUD-06) |
| 403    | email_not_verified  | Correct password but email not verified (AUD-04; only reachable with the correct password — no enumeration). A fresh verification link is emailed automatically (`sendOnSignIn`).                             |
| 429    | rate_limit_exceeded | Rate limit per email exceeded (5 req/min sliding window, FR-010) or per IP (20 req/min)                                                                                                                       |
| 503    | service_unavailable | Database unreachable                                                                                                                                                                                          |

**Rate Limit Headers** (on 429): `Retry-After`, `RateLimit-Reset`, `RateLimit-Remaining`

**Note (AUD-04)**: `email_not_verified` does NOT count toward the per-email lockout (FR-010).

---

## POST /api/auth/send-verification-email

Resend the email verification link (AUD-04, FR-029). Public. CSRF-exempt.
Always answers `{ "status": true }` regardless of whether the account exists or is already verified (anti-enumeration).

**Request Body**:

```json
{
  "email": "user@example.com"
}
```

**Success Response**: `200 OK`

```json
{ "status": true }
```

**Error Responses**:

| Status | Error Code          | Condition                                               |
| ------ | ------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------- |
| 400    | validation_error    | Invalid email format                                    |
| 429    | rate_limit_exceeded | Rate limit per IP (20 req/min) or per email (5 req/min) |
| 503    | service_unavailable | Database unreachable                                    |

---

## GET /api/auth/verify-email?token=...

Confirm email ownership by clicking the link from the email (AUD-04). Public.
The token is a signed JWT (1h expiry). Idempotent: verifying an already-verified
email succeeds. Returns JSON (no redirect — the frontend routes on `{ status: true }`).

**Query Parameters**: `token` (required)

**Success Response**: `200 OK`

```json
{ "status": true }
```

**Error Responses**:

| Status | Error Code          | Condition                                                                 |
| ------ | ------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 400    | validation_error    | Missing, expired or forged token (`Invalid or expired verification link`) |
| 429    | rate_limit_exceeded | Rate limit per IP (20 req/min)                                            |
| 503    | service_unavailable | Database unreachable                                                      |

---

## POST /api/auth/forget-password

Request a password reset link (AUD-04, FR-030). Public. CSRF-exempt.
Always answers `{ "status": true, "message": ... }` regardless of whether the
account exists (anti-enumeration).

**Request Body**:

```json
{
  "email": "user@example.com"
}
```

**Success Response**: `200 OK`

```json
{
  "status": true,
  "message": "If this email exists in our system, check your email for the reset link"
}
```

**Error Responses**:

| Status | Error Code          | Condition                                               |
| ------ | ------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------- |
| 400    | validation_error    | Invalid email format                                    |
| 429    | rate_limit_exceeded | Rate limit per IP (20 req/min) or per email (5 req/min) |
| 503    | service_unavailable | Database unreachable                                    |

---

## POST /api/auth/reset-password

Set a new password with the single-use token from the forget-password email
(AUD-04, FR-030). Public. CSRF-exempt. Revokes ALL existing sessions of the user
(`revokeSessionsOnPasswordReset`). The token is accepted either in the body or
in the `token` query parameter (same validation, no double-consumption). The new
password runs the same weak/breached checks as registration.

**Request Body**:

```json
{
  "token": "single-use-token",
  "newPassword": "new-secure-password-456"
}
```

**Success Response**: `200 OK`

```json
{ "status": true }
```

**Error Responses**:

| Status | Error Code          | Condition                                                                                                                                     |
| ------ | ------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 400    | validation_error    | Missing token, invalid/expired/consumed token (`Invalid or expired reset link`), or invalid password (min 8, max 128, not weak, not breached) |
| 429    | rate_limit_exceeded | Rate limit per IP (20 req/min)                                                                                                                |
| 503    | service_unavailable | Database unreachable                                                                                                                          |

---

## GET /api/auth/reset-password/[token]

Click-time validation of the reset link, without consuming the token (AUD-04).
Public. Does NOT consume the token — single-use consumption happens in
`POST /reset-password`. Redirects to the app origin (`BETTER_AUTH_URL`) with the
token in the URL **hash fragment** (`#/reset-password?token=...`, never sent to
the server or stored in logs); the `callbackURL` query param is ignored
(anti-open-redirect, AUD-08).

**Success Response**: `302 Found` → `Location: <origin>/#/reset-password?token=<token>`

**Error Responses**: `302 Found` → `Location: <origin>/?error=INVALID_TOKEN`

---

## POST /api/auth/logout

Terminate the current session (FR-006). Requires session + CSRF token.

**Auth**: Required (session cookie)

**Success Response**: `200 OK`

```json
{ "message": "Logged out successfully" }
```

Emits `session_invalidated` + `logout` audit events (FR-023).

**Error Responses**:

| Status | Error Code   | Condition                  |
| ------ | ------------ | -------------------------- |
| 401    | unauthorized | No active session          |
| 403    | csrf_invalid | Missing/invalid CSRF token |

---

## GET /api/auth/me

Return the currently authenticated user and active company context.
Requires session. PII access is audit-logged (FR-022).

**Auth**: Required (session cookie)

**Success Response**: `200 OK`

```json
{
  "id": "uuid",
  "email": "user@example.com",
  "name": "User Name",
  "activeCompanyId": "uuid-or-null",
  "companies": [{ "id": "uuid", "name": "Company Name", "role": "member" }]
}
```

**Error Responses**:

| Status | Error Code      | Condition                                                  |
| ------ | --------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------- |
| 401    | unauthorized    | No active session                                          |
| 401    | session_expired | Session past TTL (FR-023, `session_expired` event emitted) |

---

## POST /api/auth/deactivate

Deactivate the own account (soft delete, FR-017). Requires session + CSRF token.
Terminates ALL sessions of the user immediately and emits `session_invalidated`

- `deactivation` audit events. Data is preserved (fiscal retention).

**Auth**: Required (session cookie)

**Success Response**: `200 OK`

```json
{ "message": "Account deactivated successfully" }
```

**Error Responses**:

| Status | Error Code   | Condition                  |
| ------ | ------------ | -------------------------- |
| 401    | unauthorized | No active session          |
| 403    | csrf_invalid | Missing/invalid CSRF token |

---

## POST /api/auth/consent/withdraw

Withdraw LGPD consent (FR-021). Requires session + CSRF token. Deactivates the
account immediately and terminates all sessions (same effect as `deactivate`,
plus a `consent_withdrawal` audit event carrying `consentWithdrawnAt`).
Automated PII deletion/anonymization queue is out of scope (deferred).

**Auth**: Required (session cookie)

**Success Response**: `200 OK`

```json
{ "message": "Consent withdrawn successfully" }
```

**Error Responses**:

| Status | Error Code   | Condition                  |
| ------ | ------------ | -------------------------- |
| 401    | unauthorized | No active session          |
| 403    | csrf_invalid | Missing/invalid CSRF token |

---

## GET /api/auth/export

LGPD data portability export (FR-020, Art. 18). Requires session.
PII access is audit-logged (FR-022).

**Auth**: Required (session cookie)

**Success Response**: `200 OK`

```json
{
  "profile": { "id": "uuid", "email": "user@example.com", "name": "User Name", "createdAt": "..." },
  "companies": [
    { "id": "uuid", "name": "Company Name", "cnpj": "...", "razaoSocial": "...", "role": "owner" }
  ],
  "sessions": [{ "id": "uuid", "createdAt": "...", "expiresAt": "..." }],
  "authEvents": [{ "id": "uuid", "eventType": "login", "ipAddress": "...", "createdAt": "..." }]
}
```

`authEvents` contains only events linked to the requesting user (`userId`).
Events recorded without a user id (e.g. failed logins for unknown emails) are excluded.

**Error Responses**:

| Status | Error Code   | Condition         |
| ------ | ------------ | ----------------- |
| 401    | unauthorized | No active session |

---

## POST /api/auth/companies

Create a new company. The requesting user becomes its first member with role
`owner` (FR-014). Requires session + CSRF token. `cnpj` (≤ 30 chars, check
digits validated when 14 digits are present) and `razaoSocial` are optional.

**Auth**: Required (session cookie)

**Request Body**:

```json
{
  "name": "Company Name",
  "cnpj": "12.345.678/0001-95",
  "razaoSocial": "Legal Name"
}
```

**Success Response**: `201 Created`

```json
{
  "id": "uuid",
  "name": "Company Name"
}
```

**Error Responses**:

| Status | Error Code          | Condition                                                              |
| ------ | ------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 400    | validation_error    | Missing/invalid `name`, oversized fields, or invalid CNPJ check digits |
| 401    | unauthorized        | No active session                                                      |
| 403    | csrf_invalid        | Missing/invalid CSRF token                                             |
| 409    | conflict            | CNPJ already registered                                                |
| 429    | rate_limit_exceeded | Rate limit per IP (20 req/min)                                         |
| 503    | service_unavailable | Database unreachable                                                   |

---

## GET /api/auth/companies?q=...

Search companies scoped to membership (FR-015, AUD-10): only companies where the
caller is a member are returned (name/CNPJ of unrelated companies never leak).
Requires session.

**Auth**: Required (session cookie)

**Query Parameters**: `q` (required, non-empty, max 100 chars — partial match on name or CNPJ)

**Success Response**: `200 OK`

```json
{
  "companies": [{ "id": "uuid", "name": "Company Name", "cnpj": "12.345.678/0001-95" }]
}
```

**Error Responses**:

| Status | Error Code       | Condition                   |
| ------ | ---------------- | --------------------------- |
| 400    | validation_error | Missing/empty/oversized `q` |
| 401    | unauthorized     | No active session           |

---

## POST /api/auth/companies/switch

Switch the active company context (`session.activeCompanyId`) for the current
session. Requires session + CSRF token. Membership is re-validated inside the
same transaction as the write (AUD-13), so a removal racing the switch cannot
leave the session pointing at a company the user no longer belongs to.

**Auth**: Required (session cookie)

**Request Body**:

```json
{
  "companyId": "uuid"
}
```

**Success Response**: `200 OK`

```json
{ "message": "Active company switched" }
```

**Error Responses**:

| Status | Error Code       | Condition                                     |
| ------ | ---------------- | --------------------------------------------- |
| 400    | validation_error | Missing `companyId`                           |
| 401    | unauthorized     | No active session                             |
| 403    | csrf_invalid     | Missing/invalid CSRF token                    |
| 403    | forbidden        | User is not a member of the specified company |
| 404    | not_found        | Company not found or deleted                  |

---

## POST /api/auth/companies/update

Update company fields (owner only). Requires session + CSRF token. At least one
of `name` / `cnpj` / `razaoSocial` must be present. Empty-string `cnpj` /
`razaoSocial` clears the field. Emits a `company_updated` audit event.

**Auth**: Required (session cookie, caller must be `owner`)

**Request Body**:

```json
{
  "companyId": "uuid",
  "name": "New Name",
  "cnpj": "12.345.678/0001-95",
  "razaoSocial": "New Legal Name"
}
```

**Success Response**: `200 OK` — the updated company row

**Error Responses**:

| Status | Error Code       | Condition                                                                               |
| ------ | ---------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 400    | validation_error | Missing `companyId`, no updatable field, oversized fields, or invalid CNPJ check digits |
| 401    | unauthorized     | No active session                                                                       |
| 403    | csrf_invalid     | Missing/invalid CSRF token                                                              |
| 403    | forbidden        | Caller is not a member, or not an owner (`Only company owners can update companies`)    |
| 404    | not_found        | Company not found or deleted                                                            |
| 409    | conflict         | CNPJ already registered                                                                 |

---

## POST /api/auth/companies/delete

Soft-delete a company (owner only, FR-019). Requires session + CSRF token.
Sets `is_deleted`, purges all memberships and pending invites, and clears
`activeCompanyId` of sessions pointing at the company. Other tenants'
sessions stay valid. Emits a `company_deleted` audit event.

**Auth**: Required (session cookie, caller must be `owner`)

**Request Body**:

```json
{
  "companyId": "uuid"
}
```

**Success Response**: `200 OK`

```json
{ "message": "Company deleted successfully" }
```

**Error Responses**:

| Status | Error Code       | Condition                                                                            |
| ------ | ---------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| 400    | validation_error | Missing `companyId`                                                                  |
| 401    | unauthorized     | No active session                                                                    |
| 403    | csrf_invalid     | Missing/invalid CSRF token                                                           |
| 403    | forbidden        | Caller is not a member, or not an owner (`Only company owners can delete companies`) |
| 404    | not_found        | Company not found (or already deleted)                                               |

---

## POST /api/auth/companies/members/delete

Remove a company member (owner removes others) or leave a company (member
removes self). Requires session + CSRF token. Owners can never be removed —
not even by themselves. The removed user's sessions pointing at the company
are detached (`activeCompanyId` cleared). Emits a `member_removed` audit event.

**Auth**: Required (session cookie)

**Request Body**:

```json
{
  "companyId": "uuid",
  "userId": "uuid-or-omitted"
}
```

Omit `userId` to leave the company yourself.

**Success Response**: `200 OK`

```json
{ "message": "Member removed successfully" }
```

(or `{ "message": "Left company successfully" }` when leaving)

**Error Responses**:

| Status | Error Code       | Condition                                                                                               |
| ------ | ---------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 400    | validation_error | Missing `companyId`, or malformed `userId`                                                              |
| 401    | unauthorized     | No active session                                                                                       |
| 403    | csrf_invalid     | Missing/invalid CSRF token                                                                              |
| 403    | forbidden        | Not a member / not an owner when removing others / target is an owner (`Cannot remove a company owner`) |
| 404    | not_found        | Company not found, or member not found                                                                  |

---

## POST /api/auth/invite

Create an invite (owner only). Requires session + CSRF token.

**Auth**: Required (session cookie, caller must be `owner`)

**Request Body**:

```json
{
  "companyId": "uuid",
  "email": "user@example.com"
}
```

**Success Response**: `201 Created`

```json
{
  "id": "uuid",
  "companyId": "uuid",
  "email": "user@example.com",
  "expiresAt": "2026-08-19T00:00:00Z",
  "token": "single-use-secret-token"
}
```

**Note (AUD-01)**: the `token` (256-bit, SHA-256 hash stored) is returned **exactly once** — it is never
exposed again (`GET /invites` returns metadata only, without token). The token
expires after 7 days and is consumed on first successful accept. Emits an
`invite_created` audit event (without the token).

**Error Responses**:

| Status | Error Code          | Condition                                             |
| ------ | ------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- |
| 400    | validation_error    | `companyId` or `email` missing/invalid                |
| 401    | unauthorized        | No active session                                     |
| 403    | csrf_invalid        | Missing/invalid CSRF token                            |
| 403    | forbidden           | Caller is not a member or not an owner of the company |
| 404    | not_found           | Company not found or deleted                          |
| 409    | conflict            | Invite already exists for this company and email      |
| 429    | rate_limit_exceeded | Rate limit per IP (20 req/min)                        |

---

## POST /api/auth/invites/accept

Accept an invite by its secret token (AUD-01). Requires session + CSRF token;
the invite target email must match the **session email** (never the body).
The token identifies the invite — no `companyId` needed. Single-use:
concurrent accepts resolve to exactly one membership (the loser gets 404);
an already-member accept gets 409 without consuming the token.

**Auth**: Required (session cookie)

**Request Body**:

```json
{
  "token": "single-use-secret-token"
}
```

**Success Response**: `200 OK`

```json
{
  "id": "uuid",
  "companyId": "uuid",
  "acceptedAt": "2026-08-12T00:00:00Z"
}
```

Emits an `invite_accepted` audit event.

**Note (AUD-01)**: all failure modes (invalid, expired, consumed, wrong email)
return the same generic 404 — no enumeration.

**Error Responses**:

| Status | Error Code          | Condition                                                      |
| ------ | ------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 400    | validation_error    | `token` missing                                                |
| 401    | unauthorized        | No active session                                              |
| 403    | csrf_invalid        | Missing/invalid CSRF token                                     |
| 404    | not_found           | Invite not found, expired, already consumed, or email mismatch |
| 409    | conflict            | User is already a member of this company                       |
| 429    | rate_limit_exceeded | Rate limit per IP (20 req/min)                                 |

---

## GET /api/auth/invites?companyId=...

List invites of a company (metadata only — never tokens, AUD-01).
Requires session; caller must be a member of the company.

**Auth**: Required (session cookie)

**Query Params**: `companyId` (required)

**Success Response**: `200 OK`

```json
{
  "invites": [
    {
      "id": "uuid",
      "companyId": "uuid",
      "email": "user@example.com",
      "invitedBy": "uuid",
      "acceptedAt": null,
      "expiresAt": "2026-08-19T00:00:00Z",
      "createdAt": "2026-08-12T00:00:00Z"
    }
  ]
}
```

**Error Responses**:

| Status | Error Code       | Condition                             |
| ------ | ---------------- | ------------------------------------- |
| 400    | validation_error | `companyId` missing                   |
| 401    | unauthorized     | No active session                     |
| 403    | forbidden        | Caller is not a member of the company |
| 404    | not_found        | Company not found or deleted          |

---

## GET /api/health

Health check (FR-025, FR-028). Public. Always returns `200 OK` with per-dependency
status (graceful degradation — the process never crashes on init failure).

```json
{
  "status": "ok",
  "auth": "ready",
  "db": "ok"
}
```

`auth: "unavailable"` when the Better Auth instance failed to initialize;
`db: "unavailable"` when the database is unreachable.

---

## GET /api/metrics

Prometheus text-format scrape for the spec threat-model thresholds. Public, no
session. PII-free by design: only process stats and 0/1 readiness gauges.

```
merp_process_uptime_seconds 12345
merp_process_heap_used_bytes 98765432
merp_auth_ready 1
merp_db_ready 1
```

Gauges report `0` (instead of failing) when a dependency is down.

---

## GET /api/.well-known/security.txt

RFC 9116 security contact file. Public, cacheable (`max-age=86400`).

---

## CORS Configuration

- **Allowed Origin**: Explicit origin (e.g., `https://app.merp.com.br`) — no wildcard
- **Credentials**: `Access-Control-Allow-Credentials: true`
- **Methods**: GET, POST, OPTIONS
- **Preflight**: OPTIONS requests responded with appropriate CORS headers per FR-027


===== FILE: specs/001-auth-context/data-model.md =====

specs/001-auth-context/data-model.md: Unicode text, UTF-8 text, with very long lines (306)

# Data Model: Auth Bounded Context

**Nota (2026-10):** o schema Drizzle vive em
`server/utils/schema/{identity,company,invite,security,email}.ts`.
Todas as PKs são `TEXT` com `DEFAULT gen_random_uuid()` — o **valor** é
UUID, mas o **tipo de coluna** é `text`. Ver ADR-006.

## Entities

### User

| Field                     | Type              | Constraints                                         |
| ------------------------- | ----------------- | --------------------------------------------------- |
| id                        | TEXT (UUID value) | PK, default `gen_random_uuid()`                     |
| email                     | TEXT              | NOT NULL, UNIQUE, validated format (RFC 5321)       |
| name                      | TEXT              | NULLABLE, PII                                       |
| email_verified            | BOOLEAN           | NOT NULL, DEFAULT false                             |
| image                     | TEXT              | NULLABLE                                            |
| is_active                 | BOOLEAN           | NOT NULL, DEFAULT true — soft delete flag           |
| two_factor_enabled        | BOOLEAN           | NOT NULL, DEFAULT false                             |
| consent_terms_version     | TEXT              | NULLABLE — LGPD consent tracking                    |
| consent_terms_accepted_at | TIMESTAMPTZ       | NULLABLE                                            |
| consent_withdrawn_at      | TIMESTAMPTZ       | NULLABLE — triggers deactivation + deletion request |
| created_at                | TIMESTAMPTZ       | NOT NULL, DEFAULT now()                             |
| updated_at                | TIMESTAMPTZ       | NOT NULL, DEFAULT now()                             |

> **Note**: `hashed_password` is NOT in the `user` table — it is managed by Better Auth in the `account` table (column `password`).

**Unique constraints**: email (UNIQUE index)
**Indexes**: email (for login lookup), is_active (for deactivation queries)

### Session

| Field             | Type              | Constraints                                        |
| ----------------- | ----------------- | -------------------------------------------------- |
| id                | TEXT (UUID value) | PK                                                 |
| user_id           | TEXT (UUID value) | NOT NULL, FK → User.id                             |
| token             | TEXT              | NOT NULL, UNIQUE — session token (httpOnly cookie) |
| expires_at        | TIMESTAMPTZ       | NOT NULL — fixed 7d TTL from creation (no refresh) |
| remember_me       | BOOLEAN           | NOT NULL, DEFAULT false                            |
| active_company_id | UUID              | NULLABLE, FK → Company.id                          |
| created_at        | TIMESTAMPTZ       | NOT NULL, DEFAULT now()                            |
| updated_at        | TIMESTAMPTZ       | NOT NULL, DEFAULT now()                            |

**Indexes**: token (session lookup), user_id (user sessions), expires_at (purge expired), active_company_id

### Company (Tenant)

| Field        | Type              | Constraints                                                     |
| ------------ | ----------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------- |
| id           | TEXT (UUID value) | PK, default `gen_random_uuid()`                                 |
| name         | TEXT              | NOT NULL — company name                                         |
| cnpj         | TEXT              | NULLABLE, UNIQUE — Brazilian CNPJ (fiscal identifier)           |
| razao_social | TEXT              | NULLABLE — legal business name (fiscal)                         |
| is_deleted   | BOOLEAN           | NOT NULL, DEFAULT false — soft delete for referential integrity |
| created_at   | TIMESTAMPTZ       | NOT NULL, DEFAULT now()                                         |
| updated_at   | TIMESTAMPTZ       | NOT NULL, DEFAULT now()                                         |

**Indexes**: cnpj (lookup), is_deleted (active company queries)

### CompanyMember

| Field      | Type              | Constraints                                        |
| ---------- | ----------------- | -------------------------------------------------- |
| id         | TEXT (UUID value) | PK                                                 |
| company_id | TEXT (UUID value) | NOT NULL, FK → Company.id                          |
| user_id    | TEXT (UUID value) | NOT NULL, FK → User.id                             |
| role       | TEXT              | NOT NULL, DEFAULT 'member' — future RBAC extension |
| created_at | TIMESTAMPTZ       | NOT NULL, DEFAULT now()                            |

**Unique constraints**: (company_id, user_id) — prevents duplicate membership
**Indexes**: user_id (user's companies), company_id (company members)

### AuthEvent (Audit Log)

| Field      | Type              | Constraints                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ---------- | ----------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| id         | TEXT (UUID value) | PK                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| user_id    | TEXT (UUID value) | NULLABLE — anonymous events (failed login) have no user                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| email      | TEXT              | NOT NULL — always record attempted email                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| event_type | TEXT              | NOT NULL — one of: registration, login, failed_login, logout, session_created, session_validated, session_expired, session_invalidated, account_locked, account_unlocked, deactivation, consent_withdrawal, company_deleted, company_updated, invite_accepted, member_removed |
| ip_address | INET              | NULLABLE — retained 90 days per LGPD                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| metadata   | JSONB             | NULLABLE — event-specific data (error code, device info)                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| created_at | TIMESTAMPTZ       | NOT NULL, DEFAULT now()                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

**Indexes**: user_id, email, event_type, created_at (reporting queries)

### Invite

| Field       | Type              | Constraints                        |
| ----------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------- |
| id          | TEXT (UUID value) | PK                                 |
| company_id  | TEXT (UUID value) | NOT NULL, FK → Company.id          |
| invited_by  | TEXT (UUID value) | NOT NULL, FK → User.id             |
| accepted_at | TIMESTAMPTZ       | NULLABLE                           |
| expires_at  | TIMESTAMPTZ       | NOT NULL — purged 30d after expiry |
| created_at  | TIMESTAMPTZ       | NOT NULL, DEFAULT now()            |

**Unique constraints**: (company_id, email) — one pending invite per email per company
**Indexes**: email + accepted_at IS NULL (lookup pending invites)

### Account (OAuth / Better Auth)

| Field                    | Type              | Constraints                                      |
| ------------------------ | ----------------- | ------------------------------------------------ |
| id                       | TEXT (UUID value) | PK, default `gen_random_uuid()`                  |
| user_id                  | TEXT (UUID value) | NOT NULL, FK → User.id                           |
| account_id               | TEXT              | NOT NULL                                         |
| provider_id              | TEXT              | NOT NULL                                         |
| access_token             | TEXT              | NULLABLE                                         |
| refresh_token            | TEXT              | NULLABLE                                         |
| access_token_expires_at  | TIMESTAMPTZ       | NULLABLE                                         |
| refresh_token_expires_at | TIMESTAMPTZ       | NULLABLE                                         |
| scope                    | TEXT              | NULLABLE                                         |
| id_token                 | TEXT              | NULLABLE                                         |
| password                 | TEXT              | NULLABLE — hashed password (Better Auth managed) |
| created_at               | TIMESTAMPTZ       | NOT NULL, DEFAULT now()                          |
| updated_at               | TIMESTAMPTZ       | NOT NULL, DEFAULT now()                          |

**Indexes**: user_id (user's accounts)

### Verification (Email/Password Reset Tokens)

| Field      | Type              | Constraints                     |
| ---------- | ----------------- | ------------------------------- |
| id         | TEXT (UUID value) | PK, default `gen_random_uuid()` |
| identifier | TEXT              | NOT NULL — email or phone       |
| value      | TEXT              | NOT NULL — token hash           |
| expires_at | TIMESTAMPTZ       | NOT NULL                        |
| created_at | TIMESTAMPTZ       | NOT NULL, DEFAULT now()         |
| updated_at | TIMESTAMPTZ       | NOT NULL, DEFAULT now()         |

**Indexes**: identifier (lookup by email)

### EmailQueue (Async Email Delivery)

| Field        | Type              | Constraints                                       |
| ------------ | ----------------- | ------------------------------------------------- |
| id           | TEXT (UUID value) | PK, default `gen_random_uuid()`                   |
| to           | TEXT              | NOT NULL                                          |
| subject      | TEXT              | NOT NULL                                          |
| html         | TEXT              | NOT NULL                                          |
| attempts     | INTEGER           | NOT NULL, DEFAULT 0                               |
| max_attempts | INTEGER           | NOT NULL, DEFAULT 3                               |
| status       | TEXT              | NOT NULL, DEFAULT 'pending' — pending/sent/failed |
| last_error   | TEXT              | NULLABLE                                          |
| scheduled_at | TIMESTAMPTZ       | NOT NULL, DEFAULT now()                           |
| sent_at      | TIMESTAMPTZ       | NULLABLE                                          |
| created_at   | TIMESTAMPTZ       | NOT NULL, DEFAULT now()                           |
| updated_at   | TIMESTAMPTZ       | NOT NULL, DEFAULT now()                           |

**Indexes**: status, scheduled_at (queue processing)

### AuthSecurityState (Rate Limiting + Lockout)

| Field        | Type        | Constraints                                         |
| ------------ | ----------- | --------------------------------------------------- |
| key          | TEXT        | PK — format: `rate:email`, `lockout:email:ip`, etc. |
| count        | INTEGER     | NOT NULL, DEFAULT 0                                 |
| locked_until | TIMESTAMPTZ | NULLABLE                                            |
| reset_at     | TIMESTAMPTZ | NULLABLE                                            |
| window       | JSONB       | NOT NULL, DEFAULT '[]' — ISO timestamps in window   |
| updated_at   | TIMESTAMPTZ | NOT NULL, DEFAULT now()                             |

**Indexes**: reset_at (purge expired)

## Relationships

```
User 1──N Session
User 1──N CompanyMember N──1 Company
User 1──N AuthEvent
User 1──N Invite (as inviter)
User 1──N Account
User 1──N Verification
Company 1──N CompanyMember
Company 1──N Invite
```

## State Transitions

### User Lifecycle

```
[Registered] ──→ [Active] ──→ [Deactivated] ──→ [Anonymized] (after 5yr retention)
                  ↑               │
                  └── auto-unlock after 30min lockout
```

- **Active**: Can login, create/join companies
- **Locked** (transient): After 10 failed attempts, auto-unlock after 30min. Not a persistent state — checked at login time.
- **Deactivated**: Soft delete — cannot login, data preserved
- **Anonymized**: After 5yr retention — email/name stripped, hashed_password destroyed, IP truncated to /24

### Session Lifecycle

```
[Created] ──→ [Active] ──→ [Expired] (after TTL)
                         ──→ [Invalidated] (logout / deactivation)
```

## Tenant Isolation

Ver `specs/000-foundation/rls-pattern.md` — template de policy normativo, matriz
tabela × operação × role, assinatura `withTenant`, GUCs oficiais, fail-closed,
proibições e cross-tenant legítimo.

Resumo das tabelas auth:

- `Company`, `CompanyMember`, `Invite`: RLS por `company_id`
- `User`, `Session`, `Account`, `Verification`: Tabelas globais (sem tenant_id) — auth é cross-tenant
- `AuthEvent`: Tabela global — auditoria é cross-tenant
- `EmailQueue`, `AuthSecurityState`: Tabelas globais — `merp_app` tem DML total


===== FILE: specs/001-auth-context/plan.md =====

specs/001-auth-context/plan.md: Unicode text, UTF-8 text, with very long lines (448)

# Implementation Plan: Auth Bounded Context

**Branch**: `001-auth-context` | **Date**: 2026-06-12 | **Spec**: specs/001-auth-context/spec.md

**Input**: Feature specification from `/specs/001-auth-context/spec.md`

## Summary

Implement email/password authentication with session management (fixed 7d TTL, no refresh), multi-tenant company membership, and security controls (rate limiting sliding window, account lockout, consistent error handling) using Better Auth + Drizzle ORM on Nuxt 4/Nitro. Registration creates an account without starting a session; login creates a session. Multi-tenant membership flows (create company, invite/accept) build on top of auth identity.

## Technical Context

**Language/Version**: TypeScript 5.x (Nuxt 4)

**Primary Dependencies**: Better Auth (`better-auth`), Drizzle ORM (`drizzle-orm`, `drizzle-kit`), PostgreSQL (`postgres-js`), Vitest

**Storage**: PostgreSQL — sessions, users, companies, company_members tables

**Testing**: Vitest — unit tests with mocked feature repositories, integration tests with real PostgreSQL + RLS

**Target Platform**: Linux server (Docker container)

**Project Type**: Web service — Auth bounded context (supporting domain)

**Performance Goals**: Registration <2s p95 (SC-001), Login <1s p95 (SC-002), Session validation <100ms p95 (SC-003), Rate-limited 429 <500ms (SC-004), Unauthenticated 401 <50ms (SC-005)

**Constraints**: Sliding window rate limit (5 req/min per email), account lockout after 10 failures (30 min auto-unlock), 5s timeout for Better Auth calls, 10s timeout for OAuth provider calls, explicit CORS origin with credentials

**Scale/Scope**: MVP — email/password only, OAuth deferred, RBAC deferred, company membership via create/search flows

## Constitution Check

_GATE: Must pass before Phase 0 research. Re-check after Phase 1 design._

| Gate                           | Status  | Rationale                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------------ | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| §I Bounded Contexts            | ✅ PASS | Auth is a recognized supporting domain. Communicates via IDs/DTOs. No cross-context type imports.                                                                               |
| §II Nuxt 4 + Repository        | ✅ PASS | Handlers in `server/api/auth/`, repositories em `server/utils/auth/{identity,company,invite}/repository.ts`. Drizzle schema em `server/utils/schema/` (por feature + index.ts). |
| §III Test as You Go            | ✅ PASS | Vitest unit tests (mock IAuthRepository) + integration tests (real DB + RLS). Auth is supporting domain — tests follow in same commit per MVP shortcut.                         |
| §IV Error Handling             | ✅ PASS | `createError` for HTTP errors. FR-026 defines consistent 500 format. FR-024/FR-025 define timeout/init failure 503s. No internal details leaked.                                |
| §V Tenant Isolation            | ✅ PASS | `company_id` on all business entities. RLS + `withTenant` helper. Users belong to companies via `company_members`.                                                              |
| §VI Transactions               | ✅ PASS | Registration idempotent via email uniqueness (FR-003). Invite acceptance uses `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` row-level locking.                                                        |
| §VII Docker                    | ✅ PASS | App runs in Docker container. PostgreSQL in docker-compose. No host tooling required.                                                                                           |
| §VIII Database & Multi-tenancy | ✅ PASS | `withTenant(tenantId, fn)` for all tenant-scoped queries. RLS policy on all tenant tables. Direct `db` only for global ops (company creation).                                  |

**Violations**: None — all constitution principles respected.

## Verification

```bash
# Register a user (with optional LGPD consent)
curl -X POST http://localhost:3000/api/auth/register \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"email":"test@example.com","password":"secret123","name":"Test User","consentTermsVersion":"v1"}'

# Login (session cookie saved)
curl -X POST http://localhost:3000/api/auth/login \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"email":"test@example.com","password":"secret123"}' \
  -c cookies.txt

# Check session
curl http://localhost:3000/api/auth/me -b cookies.txt

# Export personal data
curl http://localhost:3000/api/auth/export -b cookies.txt

# Deactivate account
curl -X POST http://localhost:3000/api/auth/deactivate -b cookies.txt

# Logout
curl -X POST http://localhost:3000/api/auth/logout -b cookies.txt
```

### Multi-Tenant Verification

```bash
# Create company
curl -X POST http://localhost:3000/api/auth/companies \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b cookies.txt \
  -d '{"name":"My Company","cnpj":"12.345.678/0001-90"}'

# Search companies
curl "http://localhost:3000/api/auth/companies?q=My%20Company" -b cookies.txt

# Switch active company
curl -X POST http://localhost:3000/api/auth/companies/switch \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b cookies.txt \
  -d '{"companyId":"<company-uuid>"}'

# Delete company (soft delete)
curl -X POST http://localhost:3000/api/auth/companies/delete \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -b cookies.txt \
  -d '{"companyId":"<company-uuid>"}'
```

curl http://localhost:3000/api/_tasks/purge-sessions
